# Skills · R · part 115

871 entries, root-cause to rota-debug. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **root-cause**: Use when diagnosing a bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior before permanent remediation, and especially after a fix attempt has already failed, or when guessing has started ("maybe it's X, let me try changing it"). Triggers: 'debug this failure', 'why did this test fail', 'the fix did not work'. Human incident coaching uses `incident-investigation`; bounded sre-assistant causal assignments use this method whether direct, delegated, or synthetic. A bounded evidence lookup does not require a full diagnosis. ([latent-sre/save-toolkit](https://github.com/latent-sre/save-toolkit/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/root-cause) · 2 copies)
- **root-cause**: Use when a bug is fixed but you don't know WHY, or the same bug keeps returning. Forces past the symptom to the actual cause. ([lettland/bb-plugins](https://github.com/lettland/bb-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/devkit/content/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Find a bug's root cause from facts instead of from reading the code. Keeps a case file of facts, hypotheses and the matrix between them, steers by the strongest signal, has an adversarial reviewer on another model check the work, and refuses to name a root cause until exactly one hypothesis explains every fact. Use for "root cause this", "find out why this broke", "debug without reading the code", a bug or regression in agent-written or unfamiliar code, or after two theories have failed. Also turns a solved case into a regression or deploy gate with recheck. ([lyddonb/agent-kit](https://github.com/lyddonb/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/bstack/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Apply 5 Whys + Fishbone (Ishikawa) + Pareto when something already broke and you need to find the root cause, not the symptom. Use whenever 用户 says "为什么会这样" / "找根因" / "这个 bug 怎么发生的" / "用户为什么流失" / "数据为什么跌" / "上次也踩过这个坑" / "incident postmortem" / "事故复盘". Forces 5-level "why" probing past the first surface answer, distinguishes proximate cause from root cause, uses Fishbone's 6M categories (man/machine/method/material/measurement/environment) for systemic issues, applies Pareto 80/20 to prioritize fixes. Stops "fix the symptom" pattern. Self-contained methodology — no external docs required. ([marsloting/product-thinking-pack](https://github.com/marsloting/product-thinking-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Investigate why a metric changed or why it is low/high for some group - validate the change, find when it started, decompose the metric, drill down by dimensions ranked by contribution, and test hypotheses. Use for "why did X drop/rise", "what drives X", "why is group Y worse" questions. ([nhatruong1009/KHPTDL_AI_SKILL](https://github.com/nhatruong1009/KHPTDL_AI_SKILL/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Use when errors occur deep in execution and you need to trace back to find the original trigger - systematically traces bugs backward through call stack, adding instrumentation when needed, to identify source of invalid data or incorrect behavior ([pproenca/dot-claude-old](https://github.com/pproenca/dot-claude-old/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodology/debug/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Traces a bug backward through the call chain to the original trigger instead of patching where the error surfaces, then fixes it at the source and adds defensive validation at intermediate layers. Use when debugging an error, a failing test, or unexpected behavior where the symptom appears far from its cause. ([razorvision/clara-skills](https://github.com/razorvision/clara-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Use when a bug, failure, outage, flaky test or unexpected behaviour is found — before writing any fix. Stops symptom-patching, catches bugs hiding behind other bugs, and refuses to trust the filed description of the problem. ([Samaara-Das/agent-ops-kit](https://github.com/Samaara-Das/agent-ops-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Find the real cause of a bug before changing code. Use when something fails, crashes, returns wrong results, is flaky, or "worked before" — and before proposing any fix. Reproduce, isolate, prove the cause, then fix once. ([shankarr1503/omnistack](https://github.com/shankarr1503/omnistack/tree/HEAD/library/skills/root-cause))
- **root-cause**: Read-only root-cause analysis for a GitHub issue. Identifies the bug's location and the minimal change surface so the next agent can implement the fix without re-exploring the repo. Outputs a short summary, the files that need to change, and the proposed approach. ([zizzfizzix/agentic-engineering-framework](https://github.com/zizzfizzix/agentic-engineering-framework/tree/HEAD/core/ai/skills/root-cause))

Sauble-curated environments only — for playground / bring-your-own-source environments where you connected your own MCP sources, use the correlate skill instead.
